REVIEW RESULT
MAIN, RESEARCH-INDEX, RESULT-LEDGER, active T11 ROUTE and complete research-card161 agree on the frontier. Card160 controls only a high-squarefree-kernel energy sector and does not bound principal class1. Card161 controls only endpoints with a short primitive face hypotenuse; the complementary endpoints remain unbounded at the required scale. Neither is a whole-population closure.


Whole-population candidates exist, but no new executable saving mechanism was obtained in this review. M3-A-001 is a PAUSED sufficient receiver on the exact directional two-face host, requiring correlation decay with a one-twelfth margin. M3-A-GATE-001 explicitly requires a same-physical-measure correlation theorem or a valid coefficient adapter. M3-A-LOCAL-001 only controls known local blocker interaction, not correlation of the actual global completion predicates. Thus A001 is not an available proved aggregate estimate, and is NOT declared impossible or exhausted.


For clarity, the original unsplit directional selector table can be kept intact. Write a=M3(B), b=M2,j(B), n=N2,j(B), p=P(B), N=a+b. Its four cell counts are p,a-p,n,b-n. If F is the third-face indicator and S the space-diagonal indicator, then
Cov(F,S)=(b*p-a*n)/N^2,
and, when both variances are nonzero,
rho=(b*p-a*n)/sqrt(a*b*(p+n)*(N-p-n)).
These follow directly from E(F)=a/N, E(S)=(p+n)/N, E(FS)=p/N. They are an algebraic diagnostic of the retained receiver, NOT a new theorem or research credit: the unknown endpoint count p is still inside the numerator. Centering or applying Cauchy-Schwarz alone only yields |rho|<=1, not the decay demanded by A001. No new physical cancellation or same-measure transfer was proved.


Other retained aggregate targets remain conditional:157 needs actual same-M3 two-prime correlation control;158 records obstacles for the attempted transfer;159 does not globally exclude pre-endpoint multivariable methods; TQ-GATE-002 needs genuine global squareclass incidence, and TQ005/tH14 do not supply it merely by restricting to principal1. This review makes no literature-wide no-go claim.


DECISION AND RESUMPTION
Honor the user's stop condition: HOLD further Stage37 mainbatch production. Do not issue162 for another cutoff, a fixed curve, a renamed residual, or this review itself. Keep161 as the latest substantive research card; existing scoped results remain retained. Resume only after presenting a concrete mechanism with an original-population identity/inequality, exact R<=B, every moving family/content cost, and an actual estimate improving the full target (or controlling all remaining mass together). A new label for an unproved correlation/energy bound is insufficient. Do not automatically reactivate on a generic continuation command without first honoring this whole-population requirement.
TI remains parked;120 remains retracted;147–149 curves remain frozen. Gate OPEN and global credit0 are unchanged.
REVIEW_KIND=Within-session scope and feasibility review; not independent hostile audit.
HISTORY_BELOW=Earlier controls are retained as history; this HOLD overrides their automatic NEXT_ATTACK instructions.

# K16-C3-M3 — RESEARCH INDEX


## Purpose


This document is the navigation layer for M3 research under Stage37.


Normal restart order:


1. Read K16-C3-M3-LOCAL-TO-GLOBAL-MAIN.
2. Read this INDEX.
3. Search K16-C3-M3-RESULT-LEDGER for the intended mechanism, alias, theorem gate, or formula.
4. Open only the relevant ROUTE document.
5. Use the LEGACY-COLD archive only when the card/route documents do not contain enough derivation detail.


Do NOT begin by reading the legacy archive from top to bottom.


## Operational authority hierarchy


Level 1 — MAIN
Current goal, active theorem gate, exponent budget, current flags.


Level 2 — RESULT LEDGER
Canonical result cards. This is the first anti-duplication check.


Level 3 — ROUTE DOCS
Proof sketches, exact formulas, route-specific theorem gates, derivation details.


Level 4 — LEGACY-COLD
Historical snapshots, superseded receivers, repeated reentry audits, literature screens, and old derivations. Cold storage only.


MAIN remains the operational authority. Cards do not override MAIN if they conflict with a later MAIN state.


## Card system


Every reusable result should have one canonical card ID.


Required fields:


ID
TYPE
STATUS
CLAIM
POPULATION
HEIGHT
DEPENDS_ON
IMPLIES
ALIASES
DUPLICATE_OF
SUPERSEDES

DETAIL_DOC


Card TYPE values:


EXACT_STRUCTURE
THEOREM
RECEIVER
THEOREM_GATE
NEGATIVE_RESULT
FIREWALL
HEURISTIC_REJECTED
AUXILIARY


Card STATUS values:


PROVED_EXACT
PROVED_CONDITIONAL
OPEN
PAUSED
FROZEN
REJECTED_AS_THEOREM
SUPERSEDED


## Anti-loop protocol for AI


Before starting a new route or derivation:


A. Normalize the proposed idea to a one-sentence mathematical claim.


B. Search RESULT-LEDGER using:
- formula symbols;
- route name;
- old route name;
- likely synonyms;
- object/population name;
- desired saving.


C. Check:
- ALIASES;
- DUPLICATE_OF;
- SUPERSEDES;
- DO_NOT_REPEAT;
- REOPEN_CONDITION.


D. If the same mathematical gate already exists under another name, continue the existing card. Do not create a new route name.


E. If a negative card applies, the route remains frozen unless its REOPEN_CONDITION is genuinely met.


F. Only after this check may a new card be created.

## Promotion rule


A result belongs in MAIN only if at least one is true:


1. it changes the current active theorem gate;
2. it changes the exponent budget;
3. it becomes load-bearing for the active proof;
4. it is an exact negative result needed to prevent an immediate research loop;
5. it changes a current status flag.


Otherwise:
- add/update the RESULT-LEDGER card;
- place derivation detail in the appropriate ROUTE doc;
- leave MAIN compact.


## Cold archive rule


The legacy archive is not a startup document.


Use it only for:
- recovering a proof detail omitted from a route doc;
- checking historical wording/derivation;
- resolving whether two old route names were actually distinct;
- reconstructing provenance before promoting an old result.


If a cold result becomes load-bearing again, summarize it into a card and route doc. Do not copy an entire snapshot back into MAIN.






## Stage37 card activation router


The RESULT-LEDGER is now large enough that sequential reading is prohibited as the default research mode.


Current canonical card count at this update: 167.


For every new theorem gate, use a gate-driven rematch:


1. Normalize the live question to:
   POPULATION / MEASURE / HEIGHT / SELECTOR / REQUIRED_EXPONENT.
2. Search cards by mathematical shape, not only route name.
3. Classify candidate cards:
   GREEN_DIRECT = theorem already applies after an exact adapter.
   GREEN_STRUCTURAL = exact reusable identity/router, no power saving alone.
   AMBER = close theorem species; one explicit adapter may make it legal.
   RED = wrong measure, duplicate saving, frozen negative route, or insufficient exponent.
4. Read only the top GREEN/AMBER cards and all applicable RED firewall cards.
5. Before creating a new card, verify that no existing card has the same CLAIM + POPULATION + REQUIRED_EXPONENT.
6. Promote into MAIN only when the current theorem gate, exponent budget, proof status, or anti-loop firewall changes.


Recommended searchable structural tags for future cards:
MEASURE_TAG
HEIGHT_TAG
SELECTOR_TAG
THEOREM_SPECIES
EXPONENT_INPUT
EXPONENT_OUTPUT
ADAPTER_REQUIRED
NEGATIVE_LOCK
SAME_MEASURE_REQUIRED


These tags need not replace the canonical card schema; they are retrieval metadata.

M3-T11-023 spectral separation is retained.

Exact paired-Gaussian reduction:
mathcal_U=G_0*G_1,
mathcal_V=G_2*conj(G_1),
N(mathcal_U)=tau_1*tau_0,
N(mathcal_V)=tau_1*tau_2.

Merged tH14 closes the residue-collision component but not the global principal squareclass.

FRONTIER UPDATE AFTER M3-T11-025:
The principal torus-trace cover is K3/Kummer type, so fixed genus-one fibers and the generic Stage14 near-linear A1 target are not by themselves sufficient for the M3 fixed-content 1/12 requirement.


Exact exponent firewall:
# active tau <= sqrt(A1).
An energy route would need A1 << B^(1/6-2delta).


New direct arithmetic reduction:
W^2=-8*X*(X-Y),
X=(a^2-b^2)(m^2-n^2),
Y=4abmn.


The odd gcd d=gcd_odd(X,Y) splits into four pairwise-coprime cross-gcd cells, and after dividing by d both odd cofactors are separately squares.

## Strengthened semantic anti-loop protocol


The existing anti-loop protocol is mandatory but keyword search alone is not enough.


Before opening a new M3 card, normalize the proposed result into the following semantic signature:


1. POPULATION / MEASURE:
   exact counted objects, physical masks, height, and multiplicity convention.


2. EQUATION / CONSTRAINT:
   the load-bearing relation after removing route-specific variable names and harmless coordinate changes.


3. SAVING MECHANISM:
   e.g. square-divisor threshold, principal squareclass incidence, inverse-frequency correlation, fixed-fiber elliptic multiplicity, cross-gcd/root-line lattice count, friable support.


4. THEOREM SPECIES:
   the actual theorem needed to obtain a fixed-power saving, not the local algebraic presentation.


5. EXPONENT CONSEQUENCE:
   the exact quantity and exponent the route must improve.


SEMANTIC_NEIGHBORHOOD_SWEEP=REQUIRED.


The sweep must search RESULT-LEDGER, NEGATIVE-REGISTER, relevant ROUTE docs, and StructureRadar cards using:
- exact formulas;
- formulas after variable renaming;
- factorized and expanded forms where practical;
- equivalent squareclass / square-divisor / congruence formulations;
- theorem-species synonyms;
- receiver aliases;
- the same population and exponent target.


NEW_CARD_NOVELTY_TEST:
A new card is allowed only if at least one of the following is genuinely new relative to existing canonical cards:
- a stricter or different exact population/measure;
- a new load-bearing equation or arithmetic condition not equivalent by routine substitution/elimination;
- a new same-measure adapter that was previously missing;
- a strictly stronger theorem consequence or exponent bound;
- a proved closure/reopening of an existing REOPEN_CONDITION.


The following are NOT sufficient for a new card:
- variable renaming;
- coordinate change alone;
- rewriting a congruence as a square-divisor condition when the two are already known equivalent;
- replacing a receiver name by a more descriptive phrase;
- splitting one unresolved theorem gate into differently named copies without a new estimate;
- moving the same condition between Gaussian, rational, torus, or Fourier coordinates without a new same-measure consequence.


If the semantic signature matches an existing card, update that card or add an ADDENDUM; do not create a new card.


If uncertain whether two routes are semantically the same, default to DUPLICATE_CANDIDATE and prove the distinction before assigning a new card ID.


CARD_CREATION_DEFAULT=UPDATE_EXISTING_UNLESS_SEMANTIC_NOVELTY_PROVED.
ANTI_LOOP_SEMANTIC_EQUIVALENCE_CHECK=REQUIRED.

## Current frontier after M3-T11-041


M3-T11-041 adds an exact homogeneous refinement of the audited M3-T11-040 symmetric conductor partition.


For S=s/t and u=a/b reduced, with N_u=a^2+b^2, the split-cubic discriminant square is


W^2=-N_u*Phi,


and


Phi
=
N_u*(s^4+44*s^2*t^2-16*t^4)
-16*b*t*(a*s^3+3*b*s^2*t-3*a*s*t^2-b*t^3).


At a Gaussian-norm root a==rho*b, the cubic factor degenerates to


b*rho*(s-rho*t)^3,


with resultant


Res_a(Phi,a^2+b^2)=256*b^4*t^2*(s^2+t^2)^3.


Combining this with M3-T11-040 gives


Q_S | Phi,
M_rel^2*C_u | N_u,
M_rel^2 | W,


and the normalized exact square


W_*^2=-N_*Phi_*.


For every interior fresh prime 0<v_p(C_u)<2*v_p(m_i),


p does not divide N_*Phi_*W_*.


Thus the already-consumed conductor allocation exhausts all interior fresh valuation; do not charge another local valuation saving there.


A residual interior unit character remains,


chi_p(-rho*Pi_p)=+1,


but naive tensorization gives at most the subpower scale 2^(-omega(M_int)); a fixed-power result needs same-measure global dispersion/correlation.


The current theorem gate is unchanged.

## Current frontier after M3-T11-042


M3-T11-042 adds a collision-free single-coordinate recombination of the M3-T11-040 fresh conductor.


For the symmetric middle coefficient
E=T_2/D
one has, primewise for every p^lambda||M_rel,
v_p(Den(E))=4*lambda,
hence
M_rel^4 | Den(E)
with exact fresh exponent 4*lambda.


In audited coordinates S=s/t, u=a/b, N_u=a^2+b^2,
E=4*a*(b*s-a*t)/(t*N_u),
so the complementary S-denominator and amplified-u norm allocations of M3-T11-040 recombine into one reduced rational denominator.


For fixed rational e=E, the physical split-cubic discriminant maps to the explicit genus-one quotient
Z^2=-F_e(x),
x=u^2,
with quartic discriminant
-2^36*e^6*(e+4)^4*(3e-8)^3*(e^3-4e^2+4e+196).


The only rational singular values e=0,-4,8/3 are incompatible with M_rel>1 because M_rel^4|Den(e).
Thus every physical large-M_rel fixed-E quotient is smooth genus one and becomes elliptic over K=Q(i).


However the standard Jacobian has no generic 2-, 3-, or 5-torsion section over Q(i)(e); the corresponding division polynomials remain irreducible of degrees 3,4,12.
Therefore the M3-T11-036 Dujella route does not transfer automatically.

## Current frontier after M3-T11-042


M3-T11-042 strengthens the M3-T11-040/041 fresh allocation by removing the last valuation cap on the common-u Gaussian norm.


For u=a/b reduced, N_u=a^2+b^2 and g_u=gcd(4,N_u), the audited symmetric coefficient identities imply


N_u*T_3=4*a^2*T_1,
N_u*T_2=4*a*b*T_1-4*a^2*D,


hence


boxed:
N_u/g_u | D.


Writing d=gcd(T_1,T_3), R_u=N_u/g_u and D=K*R_u gives


S=sign(T_1)*d/K.


At every fresh p^lambda||m_i,


v_p(N_u)=2*lambda+c_p,
v_p(K)=2*lambda-c_p,


exactly.


Thus


(N_u)_{M_rel-fresh}=M_rel^2*C_u


and


gcd(N_u/(M_rel^2*C_u),M_rel)=1.


There is no fresh common-u norm valuation beyond p^(4*lambda); searching above the old cap is now forbidden.


Together with M3-T11-041, all residual fresh valuation in the normalized square W_*^2=-N_*Phi_* lies on Phi_*, with even exponent.  Interior primes have no residual valuation; extreme allocations remain the only valuation tails.

## Current frontier after M3-T11-043
M3-T11-043 closes one of the two extreme valuation branches left after the homogeneous normalization.


For every fresh p^lambda||m_i,
c_p>0 => p does not divide Phi_*.
In particular the amplified-u endpoint c_p=2*lambda is impossible as a residual Phi_* source: the exact p^(4*lambda) denominator of
E=4*a*(b*s-a*t)/(t*N_u)
forces p not dividing (b*s-a*t), while any residual Phi_* divisibility would force the same Gaussian orientation s==rho*t and hence p|(b*s-a*t).


Therefore
gcd(Phi_*,C_u)=1
on the relative-fresh support, and all residual fresh Phi_* valuation is confined to
c_p=0,
the full-S-denominator extreme.


At c_p=0, with n=2*lambda,
N_u=p^n*n_0,
t=p^n*t_0,
a first exact normalized receiver is
n_0*s == 16*a*b*t_0 mod p
whenever residual p-divisibility occurs.
The residual exponent is even, but no fixed-power count is yet proved.

## Side firewall M3-T11-044
The physical square-x condition on the retained fixed-E quartic has now been audited explicitly.


C_e: Z^2=-F_e(u^2)
is smooth genus 3 on the physical large-M_rel range.
It has quotient decomposition
Jac(C_e) ~ E_e x Jac(H_e),
where
E_e: Z^2=-F_e(x)
and
H_e: V^2=-x*F_e(x)
has genus 2.


The obvious reciprocal route to a second elliptic quotient is closed:
for the even-octic coefficients,
q0*q3^2-q4*q1^2
=
4096*e^4*(e+4)^4*((e-6)^2+12),
so no nondegenerate rational e admits a Q-rational commuting involution u->lambda/u.


This is a negative side result, not a new main counting attack.
The canonical M3 theorem gate and M3-T11-043 denominator-extreme frontier remain unchanged.

SUMMARY:
Write sigma=A_sigma/B_sigma in lowest terms for
sigma^2=|A|^2-1=Lambda(s_24)*Lambda(t)*Lambda(t/s_24).

conj(A)*U=-1+epsilon*i*sigma
turns every p^a||tau_0^sharp into an all-same Gaussian root line

(tau_0^sharp)^2|A_sigma^2+B_sigma^2
and gcd(B_sigma,tau_0^sharp)=1.

B_sigma==0 mod M_rel^2
and
A_sigma==rho_0*B_sigma mod (tau_0^sharp)^2.

TITLE=Interior residual character linearization to S-numerator and equidistribution main-term firewall
STATUS=PROVISIONAL EXACT_CHARACTER_LINEARIZATION / CRT_MAIN_TERM_FIREWALL / GENERIC_CHARACTER_EQUIDISTRIBUTION_NO_GO / HOSTILE_AUDIT_REQUIRED / NO_POWER_SAVING
TYPE=EXACT_STRUCTURE / SAME_MEASURE_NO_DOUBLE_CHARGE / GLOBAL_DISPERSION_PREFLIGHT / ANTI_LOOP_FIREWALL


SUMMARY:
On every Section-48 interior fresh prime, after exact removal of the forced conductor allocation, the residual character selector is not a genuinely three-anti-root condition: it becomes a prescribed Legendre sign of the single global numerator s of S=s/t. Across the interior support, CRT therefore leaves exactly a 2^(-omega(q_int)) fraction of unit residue classes. This proves that generic selector-only character equidistribution cannot produce a uniform fixed-power support deficit; a viable interior-character route must use additional endpoint-incidence correlation.


EXACT_CORE:
For p^lambda||m_i with 0<c_p<2lambda, put k_p=2lambda-c_p and
t=p^k_p*tau_p,
N_u=p^(2lambda+c_p)*nu_p.
Using the audited symmetric identity
P=v_1*v_2*v_3=4*u^2*S/(1+u^2)=4*a^2*s/(t*N_u),
one gets exactly
Pi_p:=p^(4lambda)*P=4*a^2*s/(tau_p*nu_p).
If a==rho*b mod p and rho^2==-1 mod p, then
chi_p(-rho*Pi_p)
=chi_p(rho*tau_p*nu_p)*chi_p(s).
Thus the physical selector is exactly
chi_p(s)=chi_p(rho*tau_p*nu_p).


For q_int=product_{p interior}p and fixed auxiliary signs epsilon_p, the allowed unit classes satisfy
chi_p(s)=epsilon_p
at every p|q_int, hence their exact number modulo q_int is
phi(q_int)/2^omega(q_int).
On gcd(s,q_int)=1 the selector indicator is
2^(-omega(q_int))*product_{p|q_int}(1+epsilon_p*chi_p(s)).
The principal term therefore has relative density 2^(-omega(q_int)), only q_int^(-o(1)) uniformly in the worst case and 1/2 for prime q_int. Nonprincipal character-sum cancellation alone cannot yield q_int^(-delta).


POPULATION:
Exact same physical Section-48 interior-prime branch inside the fixed-content/coefficient nondegenerate endpoint population with R<=B. No change of measure and no theta/Kummer 097/098 input.


DEPENDENCIES:
Hostile-audit-passed Section 35 symmetric incidence P=4*u^2*S/(1+u^2); Section 48 fresh allocation and residual selector; M3-T11-040 conductor allocation.


CREDIT:
Exact character linearization and generic selector-only dispersion firewall only. No fixed-power support saving, no denominator-extreme closure, no primary-gate closure, no endpoint theorem, no P(B)/M3(B) limit, and no Perfect Cuboid nonexistence credit.


DO_NOT_REPEAT:

CARD_ID=M3-T11-100
TITLE=Interior normalized square has no higher p-adic gain beyond the selector
STATUS=PROVISIONAL EXACT_LOCAL_SATURATION / HENSEL_NO_HIGHER_PADIC_GAIN / ENDPOINT_SQUARE_REFINEMENT_NO_GO / HOSTILE_AUDIT_REQUIRED / NO POWER SAVING
TYPE=EXACT_STRUCTURE / SAME_MEASURE_NO_DOUBLE_CHARGE / LOCAL_TO_GLOBAL_PREFLIGHT / ANTI_LOOP_FIREWALL


SUMMARY:
At every Section-48 interior fresh prime, after exact removal of the forced conductor valuation, the endpoint square equation is a unit equation whose mod-p squareclass is exactly the M3-T11-099 Legendre selector. Because p is odd, that selector is also sufficient for square-solvability at every p-adic precision by Hensel. Thus deeper p^h refinement of the same local square equation supplies no additional thinning.


EXACT_CORE:
For p^lambda||m_i with 0<c_p<2lambda, put k_p=2lambda-c_p and write
t=p^k_p*tau_p,
N_u=p^(2lambda+c_p)*nu_p.
Section 48 gives W^2=-N_u*Phi and v_p(Phi)=k_p. Hence, with
Phi_hat_p=Phi/p^k_p,
W_hat_p=W/p^(2lambda),
one has exactly
W_hat_p^2=-nu_p*Phi_hat_p
with all displayed factors p-adic units.


The Section-48 division identity and
H==b*rho*(s-rho*t)^3 mod p
give, because p|t and p does not divide s,
Phi_hat_p==-16*b^2*rho*tau_p*s^3 mod p.
Therefore
W_hat_p^2==16*b^2*rho*tau_p*nu_p*s^3 mod p,
so local solvability is exactly
chi_p(s)=chi_p(rho*tau_p*nu_p),
the same selector as M3-T11-099.


For odd p, a unit is a square in Z_p^* iff its residue mod p is a square. Thus every accepted mod-p class lifts through all p^h, with exactly phi(p^h)/2 accepted unit s-classes and two square roots for each accepted class. Across interior primes and arbitrary precisions, the exact relative density remains 2^(-omega(q_int)).


CONSEQUENCE:
The full local normalized endpoint square equation has no hidden higher-p-adic saving beyond the first-residue selector. Refining this same local equation to p^2,p^3,... is provisionally retired as an independent fixed-power route. Truly global cross-prime endpoint correlation, moving-theta correlation, and genuinely non-Kummer information remain open.


POPULATION:
Exact same physical Section-48 interior-prime branch inside the fixed-content/coefficient nondegenerate endpoint population with R<=B. No change of measure and no use of provisional M3-T11-097/098.


DEPENDENCIES:
Hostile-audit-passed Section 48 homogeneous square identity, Section 48.1 Gaussian reduction, Section 48.2/48.3 interior normalization, and M3-T11-040 conductor allocation. M3-T11-099 is only an independent selector cross-check.


DO_NOT_REPEAT:
Do not search for a fixed-power gain by imposing the same normalized Section-48 square equation modulo successively higher powers of an interior fresh prime. Once its mod-p selector holds, Hensel lifting supplies the local square root automatically.


REOPEN_CONDITION:
Reopen if hostile audit rejects the local normalization/mod-p reduction, an audited Section-48 dependency changes, or a future theorem adds global cross-prime endpoint information not encoded in the local square equation.


DETAIL_LOCATION:
K16-C3-M3-ROUTE-TRIVIAL-SPLIT-CYCLE section 110.

TITLE=Interior selector globalizes to the symmetric-product quadratic squareclass; fixed-Pi fiber is smooth genus 5
STATUS=PROVISIONAL EXACT_GLOBAL_SQUARECLASS / INTERIOR_SPLITTING_RECEIVER / FIXED_PI_GENUS5 / RECIPROCITY_ONLY_NO_GO / HOSTILE_AUDIT_REQUIRED / NO POWER SAVING
TYPE=EXACT_STRUCTURE / SAME_MEASURE_GLOBALIZATION / CROSS_PRIME_RECEIVER / FIBER_COMPLEXITY_PREFLIGHT / ANTI_LOOP_FIREWALL


SUMMARY:
The audited interior character system has a single global receiver. With M=M_rel and J=t*N_u/M^4, one has J in Z and, at every interior fresh p,
chi_p(2*s*J)=+1.
Moreover
(s*J)*Pi=(2*a*s/M^2)^2,
so [2*s*J]=[2*Pi] in Q*/Q*^2. Thus every interior conductor prime has trivial local quadratic character in the moving symmetric-product squareclass [2*Pi]; for nontrivial squareclass it splits in K_Pi=Q(sqrt(2*Pi)). Section 49's exact fresh denominator exponent 4*lambda is squareclass-trivial, so this does not produce a large field discriminant.


EXACT_CORE:
M3-T11-040 gives M^4|t*N_u. For interior p^lambda||M, writing t=p^k*tau_p, N_u=p^(2lambda+c_p)*nu_p and M=p^lambda*M_p gives
J=tau_p*nu_p/M_p^4,
hence chi_p(J)=chi_p(tau_p*nu_p).
If rho^2=-1 mod p and p==1 mod4, then chi_p(rho)=(2/p). Combining this with audited M3-T11-099,
chi_p(s)=chi_p(rho*tau_p*nu_p),
gives chi_p(2*s*J)=1.


FIXED_PI_FIBER:
For fixed rational Pi=pi!=0,
S=pi*(1+u^2)/(4*u^2).
Substitution into the Section-35 discriminant square and Z=16*u^4*Y gives
Z^2=-(1+u^2)*G_pi(u),
G_pi(u)=pi^4*(1+u^2)^5-64*pi^3*u^3*(1+u^2)^3+64*pi^2*u^4*(1+u^2)^2*(11*u^2-1)+3072*pi*u^7*(1+u^2)-4096*u^10.
The degree-12 discriminant is
2^156*pi^32*(729*pi^2-512)^3*(256*pi^4+96417*pi^2-746496).
For rational pi!=0 it is nonzero and the leading coefficient is nonzero, so every fixed-Pi physical fiber is a smooth genus-5 hyperelliptic curve.


CONSEQUENCE:
The live interior route is now InteriorGlobal2PiSplittingEndpointGeometry, not p-by-p auxiliary-sign dispersion. This is a global receiver and fiber-complexity preflight only. No fixed-power saving is proved.


POPULATION:
Exact fixed-content/coefficient nondegenerate Stage37 physical endpoint population with R<=B. Same measure. Pi!=0 on the nondegenerate carrier.


DEPENDENCIES:
Hostile-audit-passed M3-T11-099/100; Sections 35,48,49; M3-T11-040.


DO_NOT_REPEAT:
Do not re-expand the global [2*Pi] condition into independent tau_p/nu_p/rho data. Do not seek fixed-power credit from Jacobi/quadratic reciprocity alone, selector-only character sums, deeper local Hensel lifting, or the M_rel^4 denominator as a quadratic-field discriminant. Do not infer a uniform B^o(1) endpoint bound from fixed-Pi genus-5 finiteness.


REOPEN_CONDITION:

ONE_PLUS_THREE:
u=phi(t)
=-2(t-1)(t^2-2t-1)/(t^4-6t^3+12t^2-14t+11).
This is exactly the rational-root locus, a degree-4 P^1 image. Its ambient u-height count is O(H^(1/2)).


A4:
u=psi(t)
=(t^2-2t-1)/(5(t^2+1)).
This is exactly the quartic-discriminant-square locus, a degree-2 P^1 image. Its ambient u-height count is O(H).

Y^2=2(r^4-16r^3s+72r^2s^2-124rs^3+71s^4),
and modulo 5 the right side is always 2 or 3 on P^1(F_5).


Therefore, provisionally pending hostile audit, every rational u!=0 has exactly one type:
phi image => 1+3 with Gal=S3;
psi image => irreducible with Gal=A4;
complement => irreducible with Gal=S4.
